    | output "action" [1,3]| roll (rad), pitch (rad), thrust (x hover) - same as the simulator   |
    | metadata "source"    | the .zip name (printed by throw_real.py at start)                   |

4. NEXT
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    scp models\round1_final.onnx jlukas@10.160.112.138:~/Q250_Hardware_Throw_Fly/models/
"""

import argparse
import io
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

CLIP_OBS = 10.0            # same as train_imitation.py
LAYERS = ["mlp_extractor.policy_net.0", "mlp_extractor.policy_net.2", "mlp_extractor.policy_net.4", "action_net"]


def main(args):
    import onnx
    import onnxruntime as ort
    import torch
    import torch.nn as nn
    from stable_baselines3 import PPO

    with zipfile.ZipFile(args.model) as z:
        sd = torch.load(io.BytesIO(z.read("policy.pth")), map_location="cpu")
    zn = np.load(args.norm)

    class Actor(nn.Module):
        """obs -> normalise + clip -> PPO actor -> action (the whole path, in one graph)."""

        def __init__(self):
            super().__init__()
            self.register_buffer("mean", torch.tensor(zn["mean"], dtype=torch.float32))
            self.register_buffer("std", torch.tensor(zn["std"], dtype=torch.float32))
            dims = [sd[f"{n}.weight"].shape for n in LAYERS]
            self.lin = nn.ModuleList([nn.Linear(d[1], d[0]) for d in dims])
            for lin, n in zip(self.lin, LAYERS):
                lin.weight.data.copy_(sd[f"{n}.weight"])
                lin.bias.data.copy_(sd[f"{n}.bias"])

        def forward(self, obs):
            h = torch.clamp((obs - self.mean) / self.std, -CLIP_OBS, CLIP_OBS)
            for lin in self.lin[:3]:
                h = torch.relu(lin(h))
            return self.lin[3](h)

    actor = Actor().eval()
    shape = [actor.lin[0].in_features] + [lin.out_features for lin in actor.lin]
    print(f"actor: {' -> '.join(str(n) for n in shape)} (ReLU)")
    Path(args.out).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    kw = dict(input_names=["obs"], output_names=["action"], opset_version=13)
    try:
        torch.onnx.export(actor, torch.zeros(1, shape[0]), args.out, dynamo=False, **kw)   # newer torch
    except TypeError:
        torch.onnx.export(actor, torch.zeros(1, shape[0]), args.out, **kw)                 # older torch
    m = onnx.load(args.out)
    m.metadata_props.add(key="source", value=Path(args.model).name)
    onnx.save(m, args.out)

    # check: ONNX (what the Pi runs) against the real PPO's own forward pass (mean action, no clipping)
    ppo = PPO.load(args.model, device="cpu")
    sess = ort.InferenceSession(args.out, providers=["CPUExecutionProvider"])
    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    worst = 0.0
    for _ in range(200):
        x = rng.normal(0, 3, size=shape[0]).astype(np.float32)
        xn = np.clip((x - zn["mean"]) / zn["std"], -CLIP_OBS, CLIP_OBS).astype(np.float32)
        with torch.no_grad():
            a_ppo = ppo.policy._predict(torch.as_tensor(xn[None]), deterministic=True)[0].numpy()
        a_onnx = sess.run(None, {"obs": x[None]})[0][0]
        worst = max(worst, float(np.max(np.abs(a_onnx - a_ppo))))
    ok = worst < 1e-4
    print(f"max difference ONNX vs PPO (200 inputs): {worst:.7f}  ({'OK' if ok else 'TOO BIG - do not use'})")
    if not ok:
        Path(args.out).unlink()
        raise SystemExit(1)
    print(f"Saved {args.out} ({Path(args.out).stat().st_size / 1e6:.1f} MB)")
    print(f"Next: scp {args.out} jlukas@10.160.112.138:~/Q250_Hardware_Throw_Fly/models/")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--model", default=str(Path("models") / "round1_final.zip"))
    ap.add_argument("--norm", default=str(Path("models") / "obs_norm.npz"))
    ap.add_argument("--out", default=str(Path("models") / "round1_final.onnx"))
    main(ap.parse_args())

ap_link.py · all: MAVLink link helpers · 500 lines
"""
ap_link.py - MAVLink link to ArduCopter. ONE file for SITL, HITL and the real drone.
=====================================================================================
Only the connection string changes:
    SITL (direct, 50 Hz OK) : tcp:127.0.0.1:5762
    Real drone (RPi UART)   : /dev/serial0   (with --baud 921600, set later)

Observation (13) - same size as your old pipeline, but altitude-independent:
    [0:3]   rel_pos  = position - target       (NED, m)   <- was absolute position
    [3:6]   velocity                            (NED, m/s)
    [6:10]  quaternion w, x, y, z
    [10:13] body rates p, q, r                  (rad/s)

Commands:
    send_velocity(vx, vy, vz)   Stage 1 & 2  (GUIDED, NED m/s, yaw held)
    send_rates(p, q, r, thrust) Stage 3      (GUIDED, rates-only - verified by rate_test.py)
    land()                      end of every run: fly back to the TAKE-OFF point, then LAND

Return to the take-off point  (added 24 Oct 2026, Stage_3_gazebo copy)
    | Step | What land() does                                              | Printed                                  |
    |------|---------------------------------------------------------------|------------------------------------------|
    | 1    | take-off point = position when the script armed the drone      | [link] take-off point saved ...          |
    |      | (or, if the drone was already flying: ArduPilot's HOME)        | [link] take-off point = ArduPilot home   |
    | 2    | GUIDED: fly there at the current height (max 3 m/s)            | [link] flying back to the take-off point |
    | 3    | within 0.5 m and nearly still -> hold 2 s                      | [link] above the take-off point ...      |
    | 4    | LAND straight down, wait until disarmed                        | [link] landed and disarmed               |
    Not back within HOME_MAX_S (90 s) -> lands where it is (old behaviour) and says so.
    Below HOME_MIN_ALT (3 m) it lands where it is (too low to fly across).
    Heights = above the take-off point (home_height), not the raw EKF height (SITL optical flow: ~4 m off).
    land(fly_alt=3.0) = first come straight down to 3 m, then fly back (optical flow: the rangefinder must
    see the ground, max 7 m). Default fly_alt=None = fly back at the current height (Stages 1-3, GPS).
    Pilot / failsafe first: if the mode is not GUIDED / GUIDED_NOGPS / LAND (you switched to LOITER,
    RTL, ALT_HOLD ... or a failsafe did), the script stops and never changes the mode back.
    Expected (SITL): lands within ~0.5 m of the take-off point. Real drone (GPS): within ~1-2 m.
    land(go_home=False) = old behaviour (land where it is).
"""

import math
import threading
import time

import numpy as np
from pymavlink import mavutil

M = mavutil.mavlink

# SET_POSITION_TARGET_LOCAL_NED mask: use velocity + yaw_rate, ignore everything else
VEL_MASK = (M.POSITION_TARGET_TYPEMASK_X_IGNORE | M.POSITION_TARGET_TYPEMASK_Y_IGNORE |
            M.POSITION_TARGET_TYPEMASK_Z_IGNORE |
            M.POSITION_TARGET_TYPEMASK_AX_IGNORE | M.POSITION_TARGET_TYPEMASK_AY_IGNORE |
            M.POSITION_TARGET_TYPEMASK_AZ_IGNORE |
            M.POSITION_TARGET_TYPEMASK_YAW_IGNORE)
RATES_MASK = 0b10000000          # SET_ATTITUDE_TARGET: ignore quaternion, use rates + thrust

JUMP_M = 0.5        # horizontal step between two position messages (20 ms apart) that cannot be
                    # real motion -> EKF position reset; it is removed so position stays smooth
ORIGIN_ALT_M = 584.0    # EKF origin altitude (SITL default home); a label only

SETTLE_S = 15.0     # max wait after GUIDED for the EKF heading to align before flying

HOME_SPEED = 3.0    # m/s max horizontal speed when flying back to the take-off point
HOME_TOL = 0.5      # m: "arrived" = within this distance of the take-off point ...
HOME_STILL = 0.3    # m/s ... and slower than this
HOME_HOLD_S = 2.0   # s hold above the take-off point before landing
HOME_MAX_S = 90.0   # s max to fly back; after that it lands where it is
HOME_MIN_ALT = 3.0  # m: below this height it does not fly across, it lands where it is
SCRIPT_MODES = ("GUIDED", "GUIDED_NOGPS")   # modes the script flies in. Any other mode was chosen by the
                                            # pilot (RC switch) or a failsafe: land() never overrides it


class APLink:
    def __init__(self, conn="tcp:127.0.0.1:5762", baud=115200, rate_hz=50, origin=None):
        print(f"[link] connecting {conn}")
        self.m = mavutil.mavlink_connection(conn, baud=baud, source_system=255)
        if self.m.wait_heartbeat(timeout=30) is None:
            raise RuntimeError("no heartbeat - is SITL / the FC running?")
        print(f"[link] heartbeat from system {self.m.target_system}")

        self.pos = self.vel = self.q = self.rates = None
        self.armed = False
        self.mode = "?"
        self._last_raw = None              # last raw position from the FC
        self._offset = np.zeros(3)         # sum of EKF reset jumps removed so far
        self.yaw_aligned = False           # set when the FC reports yaw alignment complete
        self.home_ned = None               # take-off point (NED, m), saved when the script arms
        self.msgs = {}                     # last message of every type (e.g. RAW_IMU, EXTENDED_SYS_STATE)
        self.texts = []                    # (time, text) of every FC text message, newest last
        self.io_lock = threading.RLock()   # lets a safety thread (Stage 2) share the link
        self.set_ekf_origin(*(origin or ()))       # origin = (lat, lon, alt_m); None = SITL default
        for msg_id in (M.MAVLINK_MSG_ID_LOCAL_POSITION_NED,
                       M.MAVLINK_MSG_ID_ATTITUDE_QUATERNION):
            self.m.mav.command_long_send(
                self.m.target_system, self.m.target_component,
                M.MAV_CMD_SET_MESSAGE_INTERVAL, 0,
                msg_id, int(1e6 / rate_hz), 0, 0, 0, 0, 0)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- state
    def update(self):
        """Drain all waiting messages (non-blocking). Thread-safe."""
        with self.io_lock:
            self._update()

    def _update(self):
        while True:
            msg = self.m.recv_match(blocking=False)
            if msg is None:
                return
            t = msg.get_type()
            self.msgs[t] = msg
            if t == "LOCAL_POSITION_NED":
                raw = np.array([msg.x, msg.y, msg.z])
                if self._last_raw is not None:
                    jump = raw - self._last_raw
                    if np.linalg.norm(jump[0:2]) > JUMP_M:      # EKF reset, not real motion
                        self._offset[0:2] -= jump[0:2]
                        print(f"[link] EKF position reset of {np.linalg.norm(jump[0:2]):.2f} m "
                              f"removed (position kept smooth)")
                self._last_raw = raw
                self.pos = raw + self._offset
                self.vel = np.array([msg.vx, msg.vy, msg.vz])
            elif t == "ATTITUDE_QUATERNION":
                self.q = np.array([msg.q1, msg.q2, msg.q3, msg.q4])
                self.rates = np.array([msg.rollspeed, msg.pitchspeed, msg.yawspeed])
            elif t == "HEARTBEAT" and msg.get_srcSystem() == self.m.target_system \
                    and msg.type != M.MAV_TYPE_GCS:
                self.armed = bool(msg.base_mode & M.MAV_MODE_FLAG_SAFETY_ARMED)
                self.mode = self.m.flightmode
            elif t == "STATUSTEXT":
                print(f"[fc] {msg.text}")
                self.texts = self.texts[-49:] + [(time.time(), msg.text)]
                if "yaw alignment complete" in msg.text.lower():
                    self.yaw_aligned = True

    def request_rate(self, msg_id, hz):
        """Ask the FC to send one message type at hz (e.g. RAW_IMU for throw detection)."""
        with self.io_lock:
            self.m.mav.command_long_send(self.m.target_system, self.m.target_component,
                                         M.MAV_CMD_SET_MESSAGE_INTERVAL, 0, msg_id, int(1e6 / hz), 0, 0, 0, 0, 0)

    def ready(self):
        return all(v is not None for v in (self.pos, self.vel, self.q, self.rates))

    def wait_state(self, timeout=30):
        t_end = time.time() + timeout
        while time.time() < t_end:
            self.update()
            if self.ready():
                return
            time.sleep(0.02)
        raise RuntimeError("no LOCAL_POSITION_NED / ATTITUDE_QUATERNION - EKF not ready?")

    @property
    def alt(self):
        return -self.pos[2]

    @property
    def tilt_deg(self):
        w, x, y, z = self.q
        up_z = 1.0 - 2.0 * (x * x + y * y)
        return math.degrees(math.acos(max(-1.0, min(1.0, up_z))))

    def obs(self, target):
        """13-dim observation. target = NED position [x, y, z] (z negative = up)."""
        return np.concatenate([self.pos - target, self.vel, self.q, self.rates]).astype(np.float32)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------- commands
    def set_ekf_origin(self, lat=-35.363261, lon=149.165230, alt_m=ORIGIN_ALT_M):
        """Without GPS, ArduPilot needs an EKF origin before it gives a position.
        Safe to send every time: ignored if the origin is already set (e.g. GPS mode)."""
        self.m.mav.set_gps_global_origin_send(
            self.m.target_system, int(lat * 1e7), int(lon * 1e7), int(alt_m * 1000))

    def set_mode(self, name, timeout=10):
        self.m.set_mode(name)
        t_end = time.time() + timeout
        while time.time() < t_end:
            self.update()
            if self.mode == name:
                print(f"[link] mode {name}")
                return
            time.sleep(0.05)
        raise RuntimeError(f"mode {name} not accepted (now {self.mode})")

    def arm(self, retries=10):
        for _ in range(retries):
            self.m.arducopter_arm()
            t_end = time.time() + 3
            while time.time() < t_end:
                self.update()
                if self.armed:
                    print("[link] armed")
                    return
                time.sleep(0.05)
        raise RuntimeError("arming failed - check pre-arm messages above")

    def takeoff(self, alt, timeout=60):
        if self.armed and self.home_ned is None:
            self.save_home()
        self.m.mav.command_long_send(
            self.m.target_system, self.m.target_component,
            M.MAV_CMD_NAV_TAKEOFF, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, alt)
        t_end = time.time() + timeout
        while time.time() < t_end:
            self.update()
            if self.pos is not None and self.alt >= 0.95 * alt:
                print(f"[link] takeoff done, alt {self.alt:.2f} m")
                return
            time.sleep(0.1)
        raise RuntimeError("takeoff timeout")

    def takeoff_nogps(self, alt, timeout=40):
        """Indoor takeoff (works with optical flow AND GPS, and on the real drone):
        1. arm + climb in GUIDED_NOGPS (no position needed)
        2. hover until the EKF has a position from optical flow
        3. switch to GUIDED"""
        self.update()
        w, x, y, z = self.q
        yaw = math.atan2(2 * (w * z + x * y), 1 - 2 * (y * y + z * z))
        q_yaw = [math.cos(yaw / 2), 0.0, 0.0, math.sin(yaw / 2)]   # level, keep heading

        def level(thrust):   # thrust = climb rate: 0.5 hold, >0.5 climb
            self.m.mav.set_attitude_target_send(
                0, self.m.target_system, self.m.target_component,
                0b00000111, q_yaw, 0, 0, 0, float(thrust))

        self.set_mode("GUIDED_NOGPS")
        self.arm()
        self.save_home()
        print(f"[link] climbing to {alt:.1f} m (GUIDED_NOGPS)")
        t_end = time.time() + timeout
        while time.time() < t_end:
            self.update()
            if self.alt >= 0.95 * alt:
                break
            level(0.7)
            time.sleep(0.05)
        else:
            raise RuntimeError("takeoff timeout")
        print(f"[link] at {self.alt:.2f} m - waiting for position estimate, then GUIDED")

        t_end = time.time() + 30
        while time.time() < t_end:
            self.m.set_mode("GUIDED")
            t_try = time.time() + 1.0
            while time.time() < t_try:
                level(0.5)
                self.update()
                if self.mode == "GUIDED":
                    print("[link] mode GUIDED")
                    self.settle(alt)
                    return
                time.sleep(0.05)
        raise RuntimeError("GUIDED not accepted - no position estimate from optical flow")

    def _hold_alt(self, alt):
        """Hold xy still and gently fix height toward alt (fixes the takeoff overshoot)."""
        vz = float(np.clip(self.alt - alt, -0.5, 0.5))      # NED: + = down
        self.send_velocity(0.0, 0.0, vz)

    def settle(self, alt, max_s=SETTLE_S, after_s=3.0, alt_tol=0.1, alt_max_s=20.0):
        """1. hold until the EKF heading is aligned (first commands go the right way)
        2. bring the height back to the takeoff height (climb overshoots)
        3. hold a few more seconds.  Real drone: same messages from the FC."""
        print("[link] holding still - waiting for EKF yaw alignment")
        t_end = time.time() + max_s
        while time.time() < t_end and not self.yaw_aligned:
            self.update()
            self._hold_alt(alt)
            time.sleep(0.05)
        if not self.yaw_aligned:
            print("[link] no yaw-alignment message seen - continuing (heading may already be aligned)")
        self.update()
        print(f"[link] height {self.alt:.2f} m - moving to {alt:.2f} m")
        t_end = time.time() + alt_max_s
        while time.time() < t_end and abs(self.alt - alt) > alt_tol:
            self.update()
            self._hold_alt(alt)
            time.sleep(0.05)
        t_end = time.time() + after_s
        while time.time() < t_end:
            self.update()
            self._hold_alt(alt)
            time.sleep(0.05)
        self.hold()
        print(f"[link] settled at {self.alt:.2f} m - ready to fly")

    # --------------------------------------------- return to the take-off point
    def save_home(self):
        """Remember where the drone is now (on the ground, just armed) as the take-off point."""
        self.update()
        if self.pos is not None:
            self.home_ned = self.pos.copy()
            print(f"[link] take-off point saved: N {self.home_ned[0]:.2f} m, E {self.home_ned[1]:.2f} m")

    def _home_from_fc(self, timeout=3):
        """Drone already flying when the script started: use ArduPilot's HOME (set where it was armed).
        Converts HOME lat/lon to the local frame using the current GPS position. None if not available."""
        for msg_id in (M.MAVLINK_MSG_ID_HOME_POSITION, M.MAVLINK_MSG_ID_GLOBAL_POSITION_INT):
            self.m.mav.command_long_send(self.m.target_system, self.m.target_component,
                                         M.MAV_CMD_REQUEST_MESSAGE, 0, msg_id, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0)
        home = here = None
        t_end = time.time() + timeout
        while time.time() < t_end and (home is None or here is None):
            msg = self.m.recv_match(type=["HOME_POSITION", "GLOBAL_POSITION_INT"], blocking=True, timeout=0.5)
            if msg is None:
                continue
            if msg.get_type() == "HOME_POSITION":
                home = msg
            else:
                here = msg
        self.update()
        if home is None or here is None or self.pos is None or (home.latitude == 0 and home.longitude == 0):
            return None
        lat0 = math.radians(here.lat * 1e-7)
        d_n = (home.latitude - here.lat) * 1e-7 * 111320.0
        d_e = (home.longitude - here.lon) * 1e-7 * 111320.0 * math.cos(lat0)
        return np.array([self.pos[0] + d_n, self.pos[1] + d_e, 0.0])

    @property
    def home_height(self):
        """Height above the take-off point (m). The EKF height itself can be offset by metres (SITL barometer,
        optical flow: -4 m seen on 25 Oct), so the height is measured from where the drone was armed."""
        if self.home_ned is None:
            return self.alt
        return float(self.home_ned[2] - self.pos[2])

    def go_home(self, max_s=HOME_MAX_S, fly_alt=None):
        """Fly back to the take-off point at the current height (GUIDED, velocity commands), hold 2 s.
        fly_alt (m): first come straight down to this height above the take-off point if higher (optical flow).
        Returns True when above the take-off point, False if it could not (then the caller lands in place)."""
        self.update()
        if self.home_ned is None:
            self.home_ned = self._home_from_fc()
            if self.home_ned is None:
                print("[link] no take-off point known - landing where it is")
                return False
            print("[link] take-off point = ArduPilot home")
        if self.pos is None or self.home_height < HOME_MIN_ALT:
            print(f"[link] below {HOME_MIN_ALT:.0f} m - landing where it is")
            return False
        if self.mode not in SCRIPT_MODES:
            print(f"[link] mode {self.mode} (pilot or failsafe) - not flying back")
            return False
        if self.mode != "GUIDED":
            try:
                self.set_mode("GUIDED", timeout=5)
            except RuntimeError:
                print(f"[link] GUIDED not accepted (mode {self.mode}) - landing where it is")
                return False
        t_end = time.time() + max_s
        if fly_alt is not None and self.home_height > fly_alt + 0.3:
            # optical flow: position only while the rangefinder sees the ground -> come down first
            print(f"[link] descending from {self.home_height:.1f} m to {fly_alt:.1f} m before flying back "
                  "(optical flow needs the rangefinder in range)")
            while time.time() < t_end and self.home_height > fly_alt + 0.2:
                self.update()
                if self.mode != "GUIDED":
                    print(f"[link] mode changed to {self.mode} - stop flying back")
                    return False
                self.send_velocity(0.0, 0.0, 0.5)            # straight down 0.5 m/s, hold x/y
                time.sleep(0.05)
        alt = self.home_height if fly_alt is None else min(self.home_height, fly_alt)
        dist = float(np.linalg.norm(self.home_ned[0:2] - self.pos[0:2]))
        print(f"[link] flying back to the take-off point: {dist:.1f} m away, at {alt:.1f} m")
        still_since = None
        while time.time() < t_end:
            self.update()
            if self.mode != "GUIDED":
                print(f"[link] mode changed to {self.mode} - stop flying back")
                return False
            err = self.home_ned[0:2] - self.pos[0:2]
            dist = float(np.linalg.norm(err))
            v = err * min(1.0, HOME_SPEED / max(dist, 1e-6)) if dist > HOME_SPEED else err   # slows down near
            vz = float(np.clip(self.home_height - alt, -0.5, 0.5))   # keep the height (NED: + = down)
            self.send_velocity(float(v[0]), float(v[1]), vz)
            slow = float(np.linalg.norm(self.vel[0:2])) < HOME_STILL
            if dist < HOME_TOL and slow:
                still_since = still_since or time.time()
                if time.time() - still_since >= HOME_HOLD_S:
                    print(f"[link] above the take-off point ({dist:.2f} m off) - landing")
                    return True
            else:
                still_since = None
            time.sleep(0.05)
        print(f"[link] not back within {max_s:.0f} s ({dist:.1f} m away) - landing where it is")
        return False

    def land(self, timeout=60, go_home=True, fly_alt=None):
        """Fly back to the take-off point (go_home=True), then LAND and wait until disarmed.
        fly_alt: see go_home (optical flow: come down to this height before flying back)."""
        self.update()
        if self.armed and self.mode not in SCRIPT_MODES + ("LAND",):
            print(f"[link] mode {self.mode} was chosen by the pilot or a failsafe - the script does NOT "
                  "change it (land with the RC / RTL)")
            return False
        if go_home and self.armed:
            if not self.go_home(fly_alt=fly_alt) and self.mode not in SCRIPT_MODES + ("LAND",):
                print(f"[link] mode {self.mode} was chosen by the pilot or a failsafe - the script does NOT "
                      "change it (land with the RC / RTL)")
                return False
        print("[link] landing (mode LAND)...")
        t_end = time.time() + timeout
        last_try = 0.0
        while time.time() < t_end:
            self.update()
            if not self.armed:
                print("[link] landed and disarmed")
                return True
            if self.mode != "LAND" and time.time() - last_try > 1.0:
                self.m.set_mode("LAND")          # repeat until accepted
                last_try = time.time()
            time.sleep(0.1)
        print("[link] WARNING: still armed after landing timeout - type 'mode LAND' in MAVProxy")
        return False

    def send_velocity(self, vx, vy, vz, yaw_rate=0.0):
        with self.io_lock:
            self.m.mav.set_position_target_local_ned_send(
                0, self.m.target_system, self.m.target_component,
                M.MAV_FRAME_LOCAL_NED, VEL_MASK,
                0, 0, 0, float(vx), float(vy), float(vz), 0, 0, 0, 0, float(yaw_rate))

    def send_rates(self, p, q, r, thrust):
        with self.io_lock:
            self.m.mav.set_attitude_target_send(
                0, self.m.target_system, self.m.target_component,
                RATES_MASK, [1.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0], float(p), float(q), float(r), float(thrust))

    def send_params(self, values):
        """Set ArduPilot parameters at once (no waiting). values = {name: number}."""
        with self.io_lock:
            for name, v in values.items():
                self.m.mav.param_set_send(self.m.target_system, self.m.target_component,
                                          name.encode(), float(v), 9)     # 9 = REAL32

    def hold(self):
        self.send_velocity(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)

    # ------------------------------------------------------- record keeping
    def firmware_version(self, timeout=3):
        """Read the firmware version from the FC itself (for the run record)."""
        self.m.mav.command_long_send(
            self.m.target_system, self.m.target_component,
            M.MAV_CMD_REQUEST_MESSAGE, 0,
            M.MAVLINK_MSG_ID_AUTOPILOT_VERSION, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0)
        msg = self.m.recv_match(type="AUTOPILOT_VERSION", blocking=True, timeout=timeout)
        if msg is None:
            return "unknown"
        v = msg.flight_sw_version
        kind = {0: "dev", 64: "alpha", 128: "beta", 192: "rc", 255: "official"}.get(v & 255, v & 255)
        return f"{(v >> 24) & 255}.{(v >> 16) & 255}.{(v >> 8) & 255}-{kind}"

    def get_params(self, names, timeout=5):
        """Read parameter values from the FC. Names that don't exist are skipped."""
        out = {}
        for n in names:
            self.m.mav.param_request_read_send(
                self.m.target_system, self.m.target_component, n.encode(), -1)
        t_end = time.time() + timeout
        while time.time() < t_end and len(out) < len(names):
            msg = self.m.recv_match(type="PARAM_VALUE", blocking=True, timeout=0.5)
            if msg is None:
                continue
            pid = msg.param_id
            if isinstance(pid, bytes):
                pid = pid.decode(errors="ignore")
            pid = pid.rstrip("\x00")
            if pid in names:
                out[pid] = msg.param_value
        return out


class RateKeeper:
    """Fixed-rate loop. Records real step times so the thesis reports measured seconds."""

    def __init__(self, hz):
        self.dt = 1.0 / hz
        self.next_t = time.perf_counter()
        self.last = None
        self.dts = []
        self.overruns = 0

    def wait(self):
        self.next_t += self.dt
        now = time.perf_counter()
        if self.next_t > now:
            time.sleep(self.next_t - now)
        else:
            self.overruns += 1
            self.next_t = now
        t = time.perf_counter()
        if self.last is not None:
            self.dts.append(t - self.last)
        self.last = t

    def stats(self):
        d = np.array(self.dts) if self.dts else np.array([0.0])
        return {"dt_mean": float(d.mean()), "dt_std": float(d.std()),
                "dt_max": float(d.max()), "overruns": self.overruns}

impact_check.py · all: attitude-rule, PPO inputs / outputs (shared with Stage 3) · 891 lines
r"""
IMPACT CHECK  (Stage 3, ArduPilot SITL + Gazebo F450)   - Stage_3_gazebo copy
========================================================================

1. HOW TO RUN  (PowerShell in Stage_3, AirSim + SITL in GPS mode running)
------------------------------------------------------------------------
    Main test - compare the recoveries (~15 min):

    py .\impact_check.py --repeats 2 --compare-recovery --modes spin-stop-fast attitude-rule rate-rule

    Quick test - one hard flip, ArduPilot only (~2 min):

    py .\impact_check.py --types flip --levels hard

    All 3 types x 3 levels, ArduPilot only (~5 min):

    py .\impact_check.py


2. EXPECTED RESULT  (summary table, "Avg low" = average lowest height after the hit,
   start 30 m, higher = better; measured 2026-10-19, Test 6, about +-1 m per hit)
------------------------------------------------------------------------
    Recovery         Flip EASY  MEDIUM  HARD  | Bird EASY  MEDIUM  HARD  | Spin  | Below 5 m
    attitude-rule      22.7     19.1    16.6  |   27.0     21.9    21.4  |  ~30  |    0
    spin-stop-fast     22.9     18.4    17.0  |   26.7     24.7    19.8  |  ~30  |    0
    rate-rule (v3)     19.9     16.2    12.9  |   25.0     23.2    18.8  |  ~30  |    0
    ardupilot (stock)  HARD flip 3.0 turns: FAILED 6 of 8, lowest 0.4 - 5.1 m


    How to read it: attitude-rule ~= spin-stop-fast  -> attitude-rule is the BC teacher.
                    rate-rule is lower everywhere   -> 50 Hz delay overshoot (55-97 deg).


3. OUTPUT FILES  (folder runs\impact_check\)
------------------------------------------------------------------------
    impact_check_<date>.csv         one line per impact
    impact_check_<date>_steps.csv   every 50 Hz step (for plots)
    impact_check_<date>.json        settings, limits used, summary


4. HOW THE IMPACT IS MADE  (same in SITL and on the real drone)
------------------------------------------------------------------------
    Step 1  drone hovers at 30 m in GUIDED
    Step 2  tumble the drone until the gyro has counted the planned number of turns = the IMPACT
            HIT = "lead" (default here): attitude target LEAD_DEG ahead + the body rate  (section 10)
            HIT = "rate" (old, as in Stage_3_v1 / AirSim): body-rate command only
    Step 3  stop the tumble, the chosen RECOVERY takes over


5. IMPACT LEVELS  (same as the Stage 3 training curriculum)
------------------------------------------------------------------------
    turns = base turns x intensity      intensity: EASY 0.7-0.9 | MEDIUM 0.9-1.1 | HARD 1.1-1.5

    Type   Axis                    Rate (rad/s)   Base   EASY      MEDIUM    HARD
    flip   roll OR pitch (random)  16             2.0    1.4-1.8   1.8-2.2   2.2-3.0 turns
    spin   yaw                     10             2.0    1.4-1.8   1.8-2.2   2.2-3.0 turns
    bird   roll + pitch + yaw      10 / 10 / 6    1.0    0.7-0.9   0.9-1.1   1.1-1.5 turns

    1 turn = 360 deg. Direction random from --seed (a rerun is identical).
    Safety cap: an impact never lasts longer than 3x its planned time.


6. THE RECOVERIES  (--modes; every mode flies the SAME hit, only the recovery changes)
------------------------------------------------------------------------
    Name            Test  What recovers the drone                                   Role
    ardupilot       1     ArduPilot alone, "hold still"                             stock baseline
    limit-on        2     velocity "brake + climb 5 m/s", WPNAV limits stock        old design (did not help)
    limit-off       3     same, WPNAV limits raised                                 old design (did not help)
    spin-stop-fast  4     ArduPilot, rotation limits (ATC_ACCEL) OFF until          BAR PPO MUST BEAT
                          spin < 3 rad/s
    rate-rule       5     hand-made rule, BODY-RATE + thrust at 50 Hz               overshoots (delay)
    attitude-rule   6     same rule, sent as ATTITUDE + thrust, ArduPilot's          BC TEACHER
                          400 Hz loop does the turning                              (= what PPO will send)
    bc-policy       7     the trained BC network - run it with bc_impact_check.py   BC CHECK
    ppo-policy      8     the Stage 3 PPO network - run it with                     THESIS RESULT
                          test_stage_3_gated_curriculum.py / compare_curriculum_model.py

    rate-rule / attitude-rule (your 3 phases):
        1 arrest the rotation   2 turn upright (hover thrust while upside down)   3 brake the fall, lean against drift
    During the recovery ONLY: ATC_ACCEL off + GUID_OPTIONS bit 3 ("thrust as thrust"),
    then hand back to ArduPilot when calm (rate < 1.2 rad/s, tilt < 30 deg, falling < 1 m/s).


7. PASS / FAIL RULES
------------------------------------------------------------------------
    RECOVERED       all at once for 0.5 s: rate < 1.2 rad/s, tilt < 30 deg,
                    height > 3 m, horizontal speed < 3 m/s
    FAILED          went below the VIRTUAL GROUND (5 m), or could no longer stop above it
    RESCUE          after a FAIL the script saves the drone itself (full-thrust rule),
                    so SITL never really crashes - the hit still counts as FAILED
    REAL CRASH      disarmed in the air / on the floor: script saves, stops and says so
    Impact cut      tumble stopped early if the drone could no longer brake above 5 m + 2 m


8. SAFETY / SETTINGS
------------------------------------------------------------------------
    ATC_ACCEL_R/P/Y_MAX set to 0 (no limit) only during the hit (and the recovery for modes
    4-6), always put back to your values after - also on Ctrl+C. --keep-limits = never touch them.
    GUID_OPTIONS always put back to your value (0).
    Before every hit the drone flies back to the start point and holds 3 s.
    End of the run (also after Ctrl+C): flies back to the TAKE-OFF point at the current height, holds 2 s,
    then LANDs there (ap_link.land, added 24 Oct 2026). Printed: "[link] flying back to the take-off
    point ..." -> "[link] above the take-off point ..." -> "[link] landed and disarmed".
    If the mode was changed on the RC (or by a failsafe), the script leaves it alone.
    "[fc] Potential Thrust Loss" / "Vibration compensation" / "GPS Glitch" during a flip are
    normal: a motor is at full power while tumbling.

9. YAW BRAKE  (added 22 Oct 2026, ON by default: YAW_BRAKE_T = 0.5 s)
------------------------------------------------------------------------
    Problem found: after some pitch flips the drone is level within ~0.3 s but keeps spinning flat
    (3-5 rad/s around the vertical axis). The old command asked for "the current heading", so
    ArduPilot only braked that spin weakly; the stopwatch (rotation < 1.2 rad/s) waited ~2 s more.
    Fix: when nearly level (tilt < ~45 deg) the heading target leads AGAINST the yaw rate:
        heading target = current heading - 0.5 s x yaw rate     (at most 0.8 rad)
    ArduPilot's own 400 Hz heading loop then pushes the spin to a stop. Applies to attitude-rule,
    bc-policy and ppo-policy (one shared function, also used by training and the Pi). The lean
    direction in the world is unchanged; spin-stop-fast and ardupilot are not affected.
    The _steps.csv now also logs p_rads, q_rads, r_rads (roll / pitch / yaw rate) to show the spin.

    Expected (an estimate until tested): slow pitch flips 2.1-2.8 s -> about 0.8-1.5 s, lowest height
    about the same. If the slow cases stay, the flat-spin explanation was wrong - send the _steps.csv.

10. LEAD HIT  (added 23 Oct 2026, Stage_3_gazebo only, ON by default: HIT = "lead", LEAD_DEG = 30)
    Version 2 (23 Oct, 22:30): + ATC_RATE_FF_ENAB = 0 during the hit only (see "First test" below)
------------------------------------------------------------------------
    Problem found (Gazebo F450, flight log 23 Oct): the old hit sends only "tumble at 16 rad/s".
    ArduPilot turns that into a target attitude (the "ghost") that spins at exactly 16 rad/s. The drone
    can only reach ~12 rad/s, so the ghost runs ahead; once it is more than ~half a turn ahead, ArduPilot
    steers the SHORTEST way to it = BACKWARDS -> the flip stumbles near upside-down (rate drops to ~0,
    even reverses). Result: longer hit, recovery starts 4-7 m lower and 3-5 m/s faster than in AirSim.

    Fix: every 50 Hz step the script sends
        attitude target = the drone's CURRENT attitude turned LEAD_DEG further about the hit axis
        + the same body rate (16 rad/s) as before
    so the ghost is never more than ~30 deg ahead: ArduPilot keeps its full "spin" command and always
    pushes FORWARD. Same hit axis, same direction, same number of turns, same stop rule as before.

    First test (version 1, 22:10, hard flips x 3): no stumble, but SLOW - steady ~7.5-8 rad/s, hit 2.0-2.3 s,
    lowest 2.5-3.6 m. Cause (ArduPilot 4.4 code, input_quaternion): with ATC_RATE_FF_ENAB = 1 ArduPilot does
    not use our target directly - its ghost keeps spinning at 16 rad/s and is only pulled toward our target,
    so it settled ~100 deg ahead, where ArduPilot drops the spin command and only the angle gain
    (ATC_ANG_PIT_P 4.5 x ~1.8 rad = ~8 rad/s) is left.
    Fix (version 2): ATC_RATE_FF_ENAB = 0 during the hit only. Then ArduPilot uses our target AS the ghost
    (exactly 30 deg ahead) plus the full 16 rad/s spin command -> motors at full effort, like the old hit's
    start, but the ghost can never run away. Put back to your value (1) the moment the hit ends - before
    the recovery starts - and on Ctrl+C. A run that finds ATC_RATE_FF_ENAB = 0 at the start stops and
    tells you to fix it (left over from a crashed run).

    | Setting          | Value  | Change it                                                        |
    |------------------|--------|------------------------------------------------------------------|
    | HIT              | "lead" | "rate" = old hit (exactly as Stage_3_v1)                         |
    | LEAD_DEG         | 30     | <= 30 keeps ArduPilot's full spin command (above 30 it fades)     |
    | LEAD_FF_OFF      | True   | ATC_RATE_FF_ENAB 0 during the hit (False = version 1, slow)      |
    impact_check.py also takes  --hit rate  /  --hit lead  on the command line.
    test_stage_3_gated_curriculum.py / compare / bc_impact_check use HIT from this file (no new option).
    Training (drone_flip_recovery_env_injector_gated.py) is NOT changed - it still uses the old hit.

    How to run (same commands as before):

    py .\test_stage_3_gated_curriculum.py --model .\models\round1_final.zip --types flip --levels hard --repeats 3 --modes ppo-policy

    Expected result (estimate until tested), flip:
    | Per impact line                | Old hit (23 Oct, hard)  | Lead hit (hoped)         | AirSim hard |
    |--------------------------------|-------------------------|--------------------------|-------------|
    | hit time, 2.2-3.0 turns        | 1.58-1.85 s             | ~1.2-1.5 s               | 1.2-1.5 s   |
    | peak rate                      | 12.2-15.1, jumpy        | ~12-13, smooth           | 13-14       |
    | stumble near upside-down       | yes (rate -> ~0)        | none                     | none        |
    | lowest height                  | 6-11 m                  | ~13-17 m                 | 14-17 m     |
    The run's .json saves "hit", "lead_deg" and "lead_ff_off", so every result file says which hit was used.
"""

import argparse
import csv
import datetime
import json
import math
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from ap_link import APLink, RateKeeper

HZ = 50.0
ALT = 30.0                 # m hover height (note: a hard flip bottomed out at 3.1 m from here)
FLOOR = 5.0                # m virtual ground: below this = failed
MARGIN = 2.0               # m extra margin for the impact cut
BRAKE = 8.0                # m/s^2 braking ArduPilot can do (2 g thrust - 1 g), for the braking distance
HOLD_S = 3.0               # s calm hover before each impact
RECOVER_MAX_S = 15.0       # s: not recovered by then = FAIL
HELD_S = 0.5               # s the recovery criterion must hold
RATE_OK = 1.2              # rad/s   (original criterion)
TILT_OK = 30.0             # deg     upright
ALT_OK = 3.0               # m
HSPEED_OK = 3.0            # m/s
RETURN_MAX_S = 60.0        # s max to fly back to the start point between impacts
CAP = 3.0                  # impact never longer than CAP x planned time
HIT = "lead"               # how the impact is made (section 10): "lead" = attitude target LEAD_DEG ahead + body rate
                           #   (no stumble), "rate" = old body-rate-only hit (Stage_3_v1 / AirSim results)
LEAD_DEG = 30.0            # deg the attitude target leads the drone; <= 30 keeps ArduPilot's full spin command
LEAD_FF_OFF = True         # lead hit: ATC_RATE_FF_ENAB = 0 during the hit only, so ArduPilot uses our target directly
FF_NAME = "ATC_RATE_FF_ENAB"
_FF = None                 # your ATC_RATE_FF_ENAB value (read at start); None = not touched
OUT = Path("runs") / "impact_check"

DISTURBANCES = {           # body rates (rad/s) while tumbling
    "flip": (16.0, 0.0, 0.0),       # SITL quad tops out ~14.4 rad/s; 16 = "as fast as it can"
    "spin": (0.0, 0.0, 10.0),
    "bird": (10.0, 10.0, 6.0),
}
BASE_TURNS = {"flip": 2.0, "spin": 2.0, "bird": 1.0}       # same as disturbance_injector.py
CURRICULUM = {"easy": (0.7, 0.9), "medium": (0.9, 1.1), "hard": (1.1, 1.5)}   # same as the training
TURNS = {k: {lv: f"{BASE_TURNS[k] * lo:g}-{BASE_TURNS[k] * hi:g}" for lv, (lo, hi) in CURRICULUM.items()}
         for k in BASE_TURNS}                           # for printing / the json
LEVELS = ["easy", "medium", "hard"]
RECOVERIES = ["ardupilot", "limit-on", "limit-off", "spin-stop-fast", "rate-rule", "attitude-rule", "bc-policy"]
RULES = ("rate-rule", "attitude-rule", "bc-policy")
BC_MAX_LEAN = 0.6          # rad, clip on the network's roll / pitch target (teacher uses <= 0.45)
YAW_BRAKE_T = 0.5          # s: YAW BRAKE (22 Oct) for attitude-rule, bc-policy, ppo-policy. Heading target =
                           #    current heading - YAW_BRAKE_T x yaw rate, so ArduPilot actively stops a flat spin
                           #    left after a flip. 0 = old behaviour (heading target = current heading)
YAW_BRAKE_MAX = 0.8        # rad: largest heading lead the brake may ask for (~46 deg)
YAW_BRAKE_UP = 0.7         # brake only when nearly level (tilt < ~45 deg); while tumbling the yaw angle is meaningless
POLICIES = {"bc-policy": "models/recovery_policy_best.pth"}   # network modes: name -> file
OBS_NORM = "models/obs_norm.npz"   # input scaling from train_imitation.py (BC, PPO, Pi all use it)
_NETS = {}
SPIN_STOPPED = 3.0         # rad/s: spin-stop-fast keeps the rotation limits off until below this
RULE_VERSION = 3           # rate-rule v3. v1: 15% thrust while tilted -> 2-4.5 m below spin-stop-fast.
                           # v2: 0.4 x hover while tilted -> 1-3 m below: too little thrust to STOP the rotation,
                           #     so it overshot upright (25 -> 70 deg back) and lost height while re-levelling.
RULE_K = 6.0               # rad/s of turn rate per rad of attitude error
RULE_MAX = 15.0            # rad/s max turn rate it asks for
RULE_THR_INV = 1.0         # x hover: thrust while tilted past ~73 deg / upside down (motors need it to brake
                           #          the rotation; v2 used 0.4 and overshot)
RULE_ALPHA = 30.0          # rad/s^2: turn rate is limited to sqrt(2 x ALPHA x error) so it can stop AT upright
RULE_UP_MIN = 0.3          # "upright enough to push": cos(tilt) above this (tilt < ~73 deg)
RULE_BRAKE = 0.25          # x hover: extra vertical thrust per m/s of falling speed
RULE_SIDE_K = 0.15         # rad of lean per m/s of sideways speed, to brake the drift ...
RULE_SIDE_MAX = 0.45       # ... at most tan(~24 deg); only once nearly upright (cos(tilt) > 0.7)
RESCUE_FLOOR = 3.0         # m: rescue as soon as the drone would reach this height ...
RESCUE_BRAKE = 6.0         # m/s^2 ... braking at this rate (your SITL quad: hover thrust 0.56 -> ~7-8 max)
# RESCUE: when a test goes below the 5 m virtual ground - or is falling so fast that even braking at
# 6 m/s^2 it would reach 3 m (so it will certainly pass 5 m) - it is FAILED; the script then rescues the
# drone itself (rotation limits off + rate-rule with full thrust) EARLY ENOUGH that SITL does not crash.
GUID_THRUST_BIT = 8        # GUID_OPTIONS bit 3: SET_ATTITUDE_TARGET thrust = real thrust (not climb rate)
CLIMB = 5.0                # m/s "brake hard + climb" command for limit-on / limit-off (your AirSim action limit)
FREE_NAMES = [["WPNAV_ACCEL_Z", "WPNAV_SPEED_UP", "PSC_JERK_Z"],        # ArduPilot 4.4-4.6
              ["WPNAV_ACCEL_Z", "WPNAV_SPEED_UP", "PSC_JERK_D"]]        # newer firmware
FREE_VALUES = {"WPNAV_ACCEL_Z": 500, "WPNAV_SPEED_UP": 500, "PSC_JERK_Z": 50, "PSC_JERK_D": 50}
LIMIT_NAMES = [["ATC_ACCEL_R_MAX", "ATC_ACCEL_P_MAX", "ATC_ACCEL_Y_MAX"],     # ArduPilot 4.4-4.6
               ["ATC_ACC_R_MAX", "ATC_ACC_P_MAX", "ATC_ACC_Y_MAX"]]           # newer firmware


def rates_for(kind, rng):
    """Body rates for one impact, random sign / axis (seeded)."""
    p, q, r = DISTURBANCES[kind]
    s = lambda: 1.0 if rng.rand() < 0.5 else -1.0
    if kind == "flip" and rng.rand() < 0.5:     # tumble about pitch instead of roll
        p, q = 0.0, p
    return p * s() + 0.0, q * s() + 0.0, r * s() + 0.0


def qmul(a, b):
    """Quaternion product a x b, (w, x, y, z)."""
    aw, ax, ay, az = a
    bw, bx, by, bz = b
    return np.array([aw * bw - ax * bx - ay * by - az * bz, aw * bx + ax * bw + ay * bz - az * by,
                     aw * by - ax * bz + ay * bw + az * bx, aw * bz + ax * by - ay * bx + az * bw])


def lead_quat(q_now, rates, lead_deg=None):
    """The drone's current attitude turned lead_deg further about the hit axis (body frame)."""
    u = np.asarray(rates, dtype=float)
    u = u / float(np.linalg.norm(u))
    h = math.radians(LEAD_DEG if lead_deg is None else lead_deg) / 2
    q = qmul(np.asarray(q_now, dtype=float), np.array([math.cos(h), *(u * math.sin(h))]))
    return q / float(np.linalg.norm(q))


def send_hit(link, rates):
    """One 50 Hz step of the impact. HIT "lead": attitude target LEAD_DEG ahead of the drone + body rate
    (type_mask 0 = use attitude, rates and thrust); HIT "rate": the old body-rate-only command.
    Thrust 0.5 = hold height (GUID_OPTIONS normal during the hit), as before."""
    p, q, r = rates
    if HIT == "lead":
        qt = lead_quat(link.q, rates)
        with link.io_lock:
            link.m.mav.set_attitude_target_send(0, link.m.target_system, link.m.target_component, 0,
                                                [float(v) for v in qt], float(p), float(q), float(r), 0.5)
    else:
        link.send_rates(p, q, r, 0.5)


def send_params(link, values):
    """Set ArduPilot parameters at once (no waiting - used right before / after an impact)."""
    for name, v in values.items():
        link.m.mav.param_set_send(link.m.target_system, link.m.target_component, name.encode(),
                                  float(v), 9)      # 9 = MAV_PARAM_TYPE_REAL32


def set_params(link, values, timeout=5):
    """Set ArduPilot parameters and read them back. Returns what the FC reports."""
    send_params(link, values)
    time.sleep(0.3)
    return link.get_params(list(values), timeout=timeout)


def read_limits(link):
    for names in LIMIT_NAMES:
        got = link.get_params(names)
        if len(got) == len(names):
            return got
    return {}


def read_free(link):
    for names in FREE_NAMES:
        got = link.get_params(names)
        if len(got) == len(names):
            return got
    return {}


def quat_to_R(q):
    """Rotation matrix body -> world (NED) from ArduPilot's quaternion (w, x, y, z)."""
    w, x, y, z = q
    return np.array([[1 - 2 * (y * y + z * z), 2 * (x * y - w * z), 2 * (x * z + w * y)],
                     [2 * (x * y + w * z), 1 - 2 * (x * x + z * z), 2 * (y * z - w * x)],
                     [2 * (x * z - w * y), 2 * (y * z + w * x), 1 - 2 * (x * x + y * y)]])


def rate_rule(link, hover, full=False):
    """rate-rule v2 - the 3 phases with rotation-rate + thrust commands (thrust relative to hover):
    1 arrest + turn upright: rotate the shortest way to the target attitude (6 rad/s per rad, max 15)
    2 keep steering: while tilted past ~73 deg / upside down, thrust = hover (enough for the motors to
      brake the rotation), turn rate limited so it stops AT upright (no overshoot)
    3 brake: once upright enough, vertical thrust = hover x (1 + 0.25 x falling speed), divided by
      cos(tilt) so the UPWARD part is what we want (max 100%); when nearly upright the target
      attitude leans against the sideways speed to stop the drift."""
    R = quat_to_R(link.q)
    up = float(R[2, 2])                                      # cos(tilt): 1 upright, -1 upside down
    vel = np.asarray(link.vel, dtype=float)
    target_down = np.array([0.0, 0.0, 1.0])                  # world: body-down axis should point here
    if up > 0.7:                                             # nearly upright: lean against the drift
        lean = np.clip(RULE_SIDE_K * vel[:2], -RULE_SIDE_MAX, RULE_SIDE_MAX)
        target_down = np.array([lean[0], lean[1], 1.0])
        target_down /= np.linalg.norm(target_down)
    d = R.T @ target_down                                    # the target "down" seen from the drone
    axis = np.array([-d[1], d[0], 0.0])                      # body-down x target-down
    sn = float(np.linalg.norm(axis))
    ang = math.atan2(sn, d[2])                               # attitude error 0..pi
    u = axis / sn if sn > 1e-3 else (np.array([1.0, 0.0, 0.0]) if d[2] < 0 else np.zeros(3))
    wr = min(RULE_K * ang, RULE_MAX, math.sqrt(2 * RULE_ALPHA * ang)) * u   # no overshoot past upright
    v_down = max(0.0, float(vel[2]))
    if up < RULE_UP_MIN:
        thr = hover if full else RULE_THR_INV * hover        # tilted / upside down: keep steering only
    elif full:
        thr = 1.0                                            # rescue: full brake as soon as upright enough
    else:
        thr = float(np.clip(hover * (1 + RULE_BRAKE * v_down) / up, RULE_THR_INV * hover, 1.0))
    return float(wr[0]), float(wr[1]), 0.0, thr


def rule_targets(link, hover, full=False):
    """Shared by both rules: target attitude (level, or leaning against the sideways drift once nearly
    upright) and the v3 thrust schedule (relative to hover). Returns (R, target_down_world, thrust)."""
    R = quat_to_R(link.q)
    up = float(R[2, 2])
    vel = np.asarray(link.vel, dtype=float)
    target_down = np.array([0.0, 0.0, 1.0])
    if up > 0.7:
        lean = np.clip(RULE_SIDE_K * vel[:2], -RULE_SIDE_MAX, RULE_SIDE_MAX)
        target_down = np.array([lean[0], lean[1], 1.0]) / math.sqrt(1 + lean[0] ** 2 + lean[1] ** 2)
    v_down = max(0.0, float(vel[2]))
    if up < RULE_UP_MIN:
        thr = hover if full else RULE_THR_INV * hover
    elif full:
        thr = 1.0
    else:
        thr = float(np.clip(hover * (1 + RULE_BRAKE * v_down) / up, RULE_THR_INV * hover, 1.0))
    return R, target_down, thr


def heading_target(link, R):
    """(current heading, heading target) in rad. YAW BRAKE: when nearly level, the target leads AGAINST the
    yaw rate, so ArduPilot's 400 Hz heading loop pushes the spin to a stop instead of following it."""
    yaw = math.atan2(R[1, 0], R[0, 0])
    if YAW_BRAKE_T > 0 and float(R[2, 2]) > YAW_BRAKE_UP:
        lead = float(np.clip(YAW_BRAKE_T * float(link.rates[2]), -YAW_BRAKE_MAX, YAW_BRAKE_MAX))
        return yaw, yaw - lead
    return yaw, yaw


def euler_quat(roll, pitch, yaw):
    """Roll, pitch, yaw (rad, ZYX) -> quaternion (w, x, y, z)."""
    cr, sr = math.cos(roll / 2), math.sin(roll / 2)
    cp, sp = math.cos(pitch / 2), math.sin(pitch / 2)
    cyh, syh = math.cos(yaw / 2), math.sin(yaw / 2)
    return (cr * cp * cyh + sr * sp * syh, sr * cp * cyh - cr * sp * syh,
            cr * sp * cyh + sr * cp * syh, cr * cp * syh - sr * sp * cyh)


def reframe(roll, pitch, yaw_from, yaw_to):
    """Same lean direction in the world, re-expressed relative to another heading (exact when they are equal)."""
    if yaw_to == yaw_from:
        return roll, pitch
    fx, fy = math.tan(pitch), -math.tan(roll)
    c, s = math.cos(yaw_to - yaw_from), math.sin(yaw_to - yaw_from)
    return -math.atan(-s * fx + c * fy), math.atan(c * fx + s * fy)


def attitude_rule(link, hover, full=False):
    """attitude-rule: the SAME targets and thrust as rate-rule v3, but sent as an ATTITUDE target
    (level / lean angle + thrust). ArduPilot's own 400 Hz attitude loop then turns the drone - no
    50 Hz MAVLink delay inside the fast rotation-stopping loop. Returns (quaternion w,x,y,z, thrust)."""
    R, td, thr = rule_targets(link, hover, full)
    _, yaw = heading_target(link, R)                         # current heading (+ yaw brake when level)
    cy, sy = math.cos(yaw), math.sin(yaw)
    lx, ly = td[0] / td[2], td[1] / td[2]                    # lean (world north/east) ...
    fx, fy = cy * lx + sy * ly, -sy * lx + cy * ly           # ... in the heading frame
    pitch, roll = math.atan(fx), -math.atan(fy)              # body-down toward +forward = nose up
    return euler_quat(roll, pitch, yaw), thr


def send_attitude(link, q, thrust):
    """SET_ATTITUDE_TARGET with the attitude quaternion + thrust (body rates ignored: type_mask 7)."""
    with link.io_lock:
        link.m.mav.set_attitude_target_send(0, link.m.target_system, link.m.target_component, 7,
                                            [float(v) for v in q], 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, float(thrust))


def bc_observation(link, alt0, last):
    """The SAME 13 inputs as collect_demonstration.py (down 3, gyro 3, v_down, dalt, v_fwd, v_right, last action)."""
    R = quat_to_R(link.q)
    yaw = math.atan2(R[1, 0], R[0, 0])
    v = np.asarray(link.vel, dtype=float)
    fwd = math.cos(yaw) * v[0] + math.sin(yaw) * v[1]
    right = -math.sin(yaw) * v[0] + math.cos(yaw) * v[1]
    return np.concatenate([R[2, :], np.asarray(link.rates, float), [v[2], link.alt - alt0, fwd, right],
                           last]).astype(np.float32)


def load_policy_net(path):
    """Load a recovery network: models\\recovery_policy_best.pth (BC) or an SB3 PPO .zip (Stage 3).
    Both are the same 13-256-256-128-3 actor; the PPO actor weights are copied into it."""
    import torch
    from train_imitation import RecoveryPolicy
    net = RecoveryPolicy()
    if str(path).endswith(".zip"):
        from stable_baselines3 import PPO
        sd = PPO.load(str(path), device="cpu").policy.state_dict()
        sd = {k.replace("mlp_extractor.", ""): v for k, v in sd.items()
              if k.startswith("mlp_extractor.policy_net") or k.startswith("action_net")}
        net.load_state_dict(sd)
    else:
        net.load_state_dict(torch.load(str(path), map_location="cpu"))
    net.eval()
    return net


def register_policy(name, path):
    """Add a network as a recovery mode (used by bc_impact_check / test / compare scripts)."""
    global RULES
    POLICIES[name] = str(path)
    if name not in RECOVERIES:
        RECOVERIES.append(name)
    if name not in RULES:
        RULES = RULES + (name,)


def load_policies(names=None):
    """Load the networks BEFORE flying: importing torch takes several seconds - doing that in the
    middle of a recovery leaves the drone without commands (first BC test: it fell 30 m upside down)."""
    import torch
    from train_imitation import normalize
    z = np.load(OBS_NORM)
    for name in names or list(POLICIES):
        if name in _NETS:
            continue
        if not Path(POLICIES[name]).exists():
            raise SystemExit(f"No {POLICIES[name]} for mode {name}")
        t0 = time.time()
        net = load_policy_net(POLICIES[name])
        with torch.no_grad():                                # warm-up: first call is slow too
            net(torch.zeros(1, len(z["mean"])))
        _NETS[name] = net
        print(f"{name}: network loaded in {time.time() - t0:.1f} s ({POLICIES[name]})")
    _NETS.update(_torch=torch, _norm=normalize, _mean=z["mean"], _std=z["std"])


def load_bc():
    load_policies(["bc-policy"])


def action_to_attitude(a, link, hover):
    """Network action (roll rad, pitch rad, thrust x hover) -> (quaternion: current heading + yaw brake,
    thrust 0-1, clipped action). The SAME conversion for bc-policy, ppo-policy and the training env."""
    roll = float(np.clip(a[0], -BC_MAX_LEAN, BC_MAX_LEAN))
    pitch = float(np.clip(a[1], -BC_MAX_LEAN, BC_MAX_LEAN))
    thr_x = float(np.clip(a[2], 0.0, 1.0 / hover))
    R = quat_to_R(link.q)
    yaw_now, yaw = heading_target(link, R)                   # current heading (+ yaw brake when level)
    r_cmd, p_cmd = reframe(roll, pitch, yaw_now, yaw)        # network's lean, same direction in the world
    return euler_quat(r_cmd, p_cmd, yaw), thr_x * hover, np.array([roll, pitch, thr_x], dtype=np.float32)


def policy_action(name, link, hover, alt0, last):
    """One 50 Hz step of a network recovery mode. Returns (quaternion, thrust, action fed back as 'last')."""
    if name not in _NETS:
        load_policies([name])
    x = _NETS["_norm"](bc_observation(link, alt0, last), _NETS["_mean"], _NETS["_std"])
    with _NETS["_torch"].no_grad():
        a = _NETS[name](_NETS["_torch"].as_tensor(x[None]))[0].numpy().astype(np.float32)
    q, thr, _ = action_to_attitude(a, link, hover)
    return q, thr, a


def brake_floor(link):
    """Lowest height the drone would reach if it started braking now."""
    v_down = max(0.0, float(link.vel[2]))
    return link.alt - v_down ** 2 / (2 * BRAKE)


def crashed(link):
    return (not link.armed) or link.alt < 0.3


def state(link, target):
    tilt = link.tilt_deg
    rate = float(np.linalg.norm(link.rates))
    hspeed = float(np.linalg.norm(link.vel[0:2]))
    alt = link.alt
    ok = rate < RATE_OK and tilt < TILT_OK and alt > ALT_OK and hspeed < HSPEED_OK
    return {"tilt": tilt, "rate": rate, "hspeed": hspeed, "alt": alt, "pqr": [float(v) for v in link.rates],
            "dist": float(np.linalg.norm(link.pos - target)), "ok": ok}


def fly_back(link, target, hold_s, max_s=RETURN_MAX_S):
    """Fly back to the start point (gentle velocity commands), then hold there calmly for hold_s."""
    rk = RateKeeper(HZ)
    t0, calm_since = time.time(), None
    while time.time() - t0 < max_s:
        link.update()
        err = target - link.pos
        v = np.clip(0.8 * err, -2.0, 2.0)
        link.send_velocity(v[0], v[1], float(np.clip(v[2], -1.5, 1.5)))
        rk.wait()
        calm = np.linalg.norm(err) < 0.5 and np.linalg.norm(link.vel) < 0.3 and link.tilt_deg < 5
        calm_since = (calm_since or time.time()) if calm else None
        if calm_since and time.time() - calm_since >= hold_s:
            return True
    print("   (did not get back to the start point calmly - continuing)")
    return False


def log_row(rows, n, kind, level, phase, t, s, turns, recovery="ardupilot"):
    rows.append([n, kind, level, phase, round(t, 3), round(s["tilt"], 1), round(s["rate"], 2),
                 round(turns, 2), round(s["alt"], 2), round(s["hspeed"], 2), round(s["dist"], 2), recovery]
                + [round(v, 2) for v in s["pqr"]])                # body rates p, q, r (roll / pitch / YAW)


def one_impact(link, kind, level, rates, n, rows, target, limits, recovery="ardupilot", free=None,
               guid=None, hover=0.4, goal=None):
    fly_back(link, target, HOLD_S)
    p, q, r = rates
    planned = goal * 2 * math.pi / float(np.linalg.norm([p, q, r]))
    alt0 = link.alt
    peak_tilt = peak_rate = turned = 0.0
    cut = False
    if limits:
        send_params(link, {k: 0 for k in limits})       # impact mode on
    if _FF is not None:
        send_params(link, {FF_NAME: 0})                  # lead hit: ArduPilot uses our target as its ghost
    if recovery == "limit-off":
        send_params(link, {k: FREE_VALUES[k] for k in free})   # loosen BEFORE velocity control restarts
    rk = RateKeeper(HZ)
    t0 = t_prev = time.time()
    # ---- impact: tumble until the gyro has counted the planned turns
    while turned < goal and time.time() - t0 < CAP * planned:
        send_hit(link, (p, q, r))
        rk.wait()
        link.update()
        now = time.time()
        turned += float(np.linalg.norm(link.rates)) * (now - t_prev) / (2 * math.pi)
        t_prev = now
        s = state(link, target)
        peak_tilt, peak_rate = max(peak_tilt, s["tilt"]), max(peak_rate, s["rate"])
        log_row(rows, n, kind, level, "impact", now - t0, s, turned, recovery)
        if brake_floor(link) < FLOOR + MARGIN:
            cut = True
            break
        if crashed(link):
            raise SystemExit("💥 REAL CRASH during the impact (disarmed or on the floor) - "
                             "results saved; restart SITL")
    impact_s = time.time() - t0
    if _FF is not None:
        send_params(link, {FF_NAME: _FF})                # your value back BEFORE the recovery starts
    keep_rot = recovery in ("spin-stop-fast",) + RULES and bool(limits)
    if limits and not keep_rot:
        send_params(link, limits)                        # stock ArduPilot for the recovery
    rule_on = recovery in RULES
    if rule_on:
        send_params(link, {"GUID_OPTIONS": int(guid) | GUID_THRUST_BIT})   # thrust field = real thrust
    spin_stop_t = rule_end_t = None
    rescue = False
    t_rel = time.time()
    # ---- recovery: ardupilot = hold still (baseline); limit-on / limit-off = brake + climb hard
    rec_t, held_since, min_alt, ground = None, None, link.alt, False
    alt_end_impact = link.alt
    bc_last = np.array([0.0, 0.0, 1.0], dtype=np.float32)
    while time.time() - t_rel < RECOVER_MAX_S:
        if rule_on:
            if recovery == "attitude-rule" and not rescue:
                send_attitude(link, *attitude_rule(link, hover))
            elif recovery in POLICIES and not rescue:
                q_bc, thr_bc, bc_last = policy_action(recovery, link, hover, alt0, bc_last)
                send_attitude(link, q_bc, thr_bc)
            else:
                link.send_rates(*rate_rule(link, hover, full=rescue))
        elif recovery in ("ardupilot", "spin-stop-fast") or link.alt >= ALT:
            link.send_velocity(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)
        else:
            link.send_velocity(0.0, 0.0, -CLIMB)          # NED: negative = up
        rk.wait()
        link.update()
        s = state(link, target)
        peak_tilt, peak_rate = max(peak_tilt, s["tilt"]), max(peak_rate, s["rate"])
        min_alt = min(min_alt, s["alt"])
        ground = ground or s["alt"] < FLOOR
        doomed = s["alt"] - max(0.0, float(link.vel[2])) ** 2 / (2 * RESCUE_BRAKE) < RESCUE_FLOOR
        if doomed and not rescue:
            ground = True                                    # would pass 5 m even braking hard = FAILED
        if ground and not rescue and guid is not None and limits:
            rescue = rule_on = keep_rot = True               # already FAILED - now save the drone
            send_params(link, {**{k: 0 for k in limits}, "GUID_OPTIONS": int(guid) | GUID_THRUST_BIT})
        if crashed(link):
            raise SystemExit("💥 REAL CRASH during recovery (disarmed or on the floor) - "
                             "results saved; restart SITL")
        log_row(rows, n, kind, level, "recovery", time.time() - t0, s, turned, recovery)
        if spin_stop_t is None and s["rate"] < SPIN_STOPPED:
            spin_stop_t = time.time() - t_rel
        if rule_on and s["rate"] < RATE_OK and s["tilt"] < TILT_OK and float(link.vel[2]) < 1.0:
            rule_on = False                              # calm: hand back to ArduPilot (velocity hold)
            rule_end_t = rule_end_t if rescue else time.time() - t_rel
            send_params(link, {"GUID_OPTIONS": int(guid)})
        if keep_rot and ((recovery == "spin-stop-fast" and s["rate"] < SPIN_STOPPED) or
                         ((recovery in RULES or rescue) and not rule_on)):
            send_params(link, limits)                    # stock rotation limits again
            keep_rot = False
        if s["ok"]:
            held_since = held_since or time.time()
            if time.time() - held_since >= HELD_S:
                rec_t = held_since - t_rel
                break
        else:
            held_since = None
    if recovery == "limit-off":
        send_params(link, free)                          # stock limits again
    if keep_rot:
        send_params(link, limits)
    if rule_on:
        send_params(link, {"GUID_OPTIONS": int(guid)})
    link.update()
    info = {"n": n, "type": kind, "level": level, "recovery": recovery, "cmd_rates": [round(p, 2), round(q, 2), round(r, 2)],
            "planned_turns": round(goal, 2), "turns": round(turned, 2), "impact_s": round(impact_s, 2),
            "peak_tilt_deg": round(peak_tilt, 1), "peak_rate_rads": round(peak_rate, 2),
            "recovered": bool(rec_t is not None and not ground),
            "recovery_time_s": round(rec_t, 2) if rec_t is not None else None,
            "alt_loss_m": round(alt0 - min_alt, 2), "min_alt_m": round(min_alt, 2), "virtual_ground": bool(ground),
            "drift_m": round(float(np.linalg.norm(link.pos - target)), 2), "impact_cut_low": bool(cut),
            "spin_stopped_s": round(spin_stop_t, 2) if spin_stop_t is not None else None,
            "alt_end_impact_m": round(alt_end_impact, 2),
            "rule_handover_s": round(rule_end_t, 2) if rule_end_t is not None else None,
            "rescued": bool(rescue)}
    fly_back(link, target, 0.0)
    return info


def main(args):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(args.seed)
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("💥 STAGE 3 IMPACT CHECK - does the disturbance work in SITL?")
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"Types: {', '.join(args.types)} | Levels: {', '.join(args.levels)} | repeats: {args.repeats}")
    print(f"Hit method: {HIT}" + (f" (attitude target {LEAD_DEG:g} deg ahead + body rate)" if HIT == "lead"
                                   else " (body-rate command only - old)"))
    for k in args.types:
        print(f"   {k:<5} rates {DISTURBANCES[k]} rad/s | turns " +
              ", ".join(f"{lv} {TURNS[k][lv]}" for lv in args.levels) + " turns")
    modes = (args.modes or RECOVERIES) if args.compare_recovery else ["ardupilot"]
    if args.compare_recovery:
        print(f"Compare recovery: every impact flown {len(modes)}x - {', '.join(modes)} (same hit each time)")
    print(f"Recovery (baseline): ArduPilot GUIDED hold | criterion: rate < {RATE_OK} rad/s, "
          f"tilt < {TILT_OK:.0f} deg, alt > {ALT_OK:.0f} m, h-speed < {HSPEED_OK:.0f} m/s for {HELD_S} s")
    print()
    nets = [m for m in modes if m in POLICIES]
    if nets:
        load_policies(nets)                              # BEFORE connecting / takeoff, never mid-recovery
    link = APLink(args.conn, rate_hz=50)
    link.wait_state()
    original = read_limits(link)
    stuck = {k: v for k, v in original.items() if abs(v) < 1e-6}
    g0 = link.get_params(["GUID_OPTIONS"]).get("GUID_OPTIONS")
    if stuck or (g0 is not None and int(g0) & GUID_THRUST_BIT):
        raise SystemExit("STOP - the FC still has test values from a crashed run: "
                         f"{stuck or ''} {'GUID_OPTIONS ' + str(int(g0)) if g0 is not None and int(g0) & GUID_THRUST_BIT else ''}\n"
                         "Fix in the MAVProxy / SITL console, then run again:\n"
                         "    param set ATC_ACCEL_R_MAX 110000\n    param set ATC_ACCEL_P_MAX 110000\n"
                         "    param set ATC_ACCEL_Y_MAX 27000\n    param set GUID_OPTIONS 0")
    limits = {} if args.keep_limits else dict(original)
    if not args.keep_limits:
        if not original:
            raise SystemExit("Could not read the ATC_ACCEL/ATC_ACC limits - not changing anything "
                             "(run with --keep-limits to test without impact mode)")
        print(f"⚙️  Impact mode: {', '.join(f'{k} {v:g}' for k, v in original.items())} -> 0 (no limit) "
              "only during each impact")
        test = set_params(link, {k: 0 for k in original})
        ok = all(abs(v) < 1e-6 for v in test.values()) and len(test) == len(original)
        back = set_params(link, original)
        print(f"   check: set to 0 -> FC reports {test} | put back -> {back} | {'OK' if ok else 'FAILED'}")
        if not ok:
            raise SystemExit("The FC did not accept the limit change - stopping")
    global _FF
    _FF = None
    if HIT == "lead" and LEAD_FF_OFF and limits:
        ff = link.get_params([FF_NAME]).get(FF_NAME)
        if ff is None:
            raise SystemExit(f"Could not read {FF_NAME} - needed for the lead hit (or run with --hit rate)")
        if int(ff) == 0:
            raise SystemExit(f"STOP - {FF_NAME} is 0 (left over from a crashed lead-hit run?). Fix in MAVProxy:\n"
                             f"    param set {FF_NAME} 1")
        test = set_params(link, {FF_NAME: 0})
        back = set_params(link, {FF_NAME: ff})
        if abs(test.get(FF_NAME, 1)) > 1e-6 or abs(back.get(FF_NAME, -1) - ff) > 1e-6:
            raise SystemExit(f"The FC did not accept the {FF_NAME} change - stopping")
        _FF = float(ff)
        print(f"⚙️  Lead hit: {FF_NAME} {ff:g} -> 0 only during each hit (check: OK)")
    free, guid, hover = {}, None, 0.4
    got = link.get_params(["GUID_OPTIONS", "MOT_THST_HOVER"])
    if "GUID_OPTIONS" in got:
        guid = int(got["GUID_OPTIONS"])
        hover = float(got.get("MOT_THST_HOVER", 0.4))
    if any(m in RULES for m in modes) and (guid is None or not limits):
        raise SystemExit("rate-rule needs GUID_OPTIONS readable and impact mode (no --keep-limits)")
    if guid is not None and limits:
        print(f"⚙️  rate-rule / rescue: GUID_OPTIONS {guid} -> {guid | GUID_THRUST_BIT} (thrust as thrust) only "
              f"until calm | hover thrust {hover:.2f}")
        print(f"🛟 Rescue ON: a test that goes below {FLOOR:g} m (or falls too fast to stop above it) is recorded "
              "as FAILED, then the script saves the drone early (no more SITL restarts)")
    else:
        print("⚠️  Rescue OFF (GUID_OPTIONS not readable or --keep-limits) - a bad fall can crash SITL")
    if "limit-off" in modes:
        free = read_free(link)
        if not free:
            raise SystemExit("Could not read WPNAV_ACCEL_Z / WPNAV_SPEED_UP / PSC_JERK_Z - cannot run the limit-off test")
        test = set_params(link, {k: FREE_VALUES[k] for k in free})
        back = set_params(link, free)
        ok = len(test) == len(free) and all(abs(test[k] - FREE_VALUES[k]) < 1e-3 for k in free)
        print(f"⚙️  Brake limit OFF (limit-off test): {', '.join(f'{k} {v:g}' for k, v in free.items())} -> "
              f"{', '.join(f'{k} {FREE_VALUES[k]:g}' for k in free)} (only from the hit until recovered)")
        print(f"   check: loosened -> FC reports {test} | put back -> {back} | {'OK' if ok else 'FAILED'}")
        if not ok:
            raise SystemExit("The FC did not accept brake limit OFF - stopping")
    link.update()
    OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    stamp = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    base = OUT / f"{OUT.name}_{stamp}"
    results, rows, n = [], [], 0
    try:
        if not link.armed:
            link.takeoff_nogps(ALT)
        link.update()
        target = link.pos.copy()
        target[2] = -ALT
        print("=" * 70)
        for kind in args.types:
            for level in args.levels:
                for _ in range(args.repeats):
                    rates = rates_for(kind, rng)            # the same hit for every recovery mode
                    goal = BASE_TURNS[kind] * rng.uniform(*CURRICULUM[level])
                    for mode in modes:
                        n += 1
                        e = one_impact(link, kind, level, rates, n, rows, target, limits, mode, free, guid, hover,
                                       goal)
                        results.append(e)
                        spin = (f" | spin stopped {e['spin_stopped_s']:.2f}s after hit"
                                if e["spin_stopped_s"] is not None else " | spin not stopped")
                        rec = (f"recovered {e['recovery_time_s']:.2f}s" if e["recovered"] else
                               "FAILED - hit virtual ground" if e["virtual_ground"] else "FAILED - not recovered")
                        tag = f" | {mode:<14}" if args.compare_recovery else ""
                        print(f"Impact {n:2d} | {kind:<4} {level:<6}{tag} | turns {e['turns']:.1f}/{e['planned_turns']:.1f} "
                              f"in {e['impact_s']:.2f}s | peak rate {e['peak_rate_rads']:4.1f} rad/s | "
                              f"height loss {e['alt_loss_m']:4.1f} m (lowest {e['min_alt_m']:4.1f} m) | {rec}"
                              + spin + (" | impact cut early (braking)" if e["impact_cut_low"] else "")
                              + (" | rescued by the script below 5 m" if e["rescued"] else ""))
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nStopped by user - saving what we have")
    finally:
        print("=" * 70)
        if limits:
            back = set_params(link, original)
            print(f"⚙️  Limits restored: {back}")
        if _FF is not None:
            back = set_params(link, {FF_NAME: _FF})
            print(f"⚙️  {FF_NAME} restored: {back}")
        if free:
            back = set_params(link, free)
            print(f"⚙️  Brake limit back ON (restored): {back}")
        if guid is not None and limits:
            back = set_params(link, {"GUID_OPTIONS": guid})
            print(f"⚙️  GUID_OPTIONS restored: {back}")
        cols = ["n", "type", "level", "recovery", "cmd_rates", "planned_turns", "turns", "impact_s", "peak_rate_rads",
                "peak_tilt_deg", "recovered", "recovery_time_s", "alt_loss_m", "min_alt_m", "virtual_ground", "drift_m",
                "impact_cut_low", "alt_end_impact_m", "spin_stopped_s", "rule_handover_s", "rescued"]
        with open(f"{base}.csv", "w", newline="") as f:
            w = csv.writer(f)
            w.writerow(cols)
            for e in results:
                w.writerow([e[c] for c in cols])
        with open(f"{base}_steps.csv", "w", newline="") as f:
            w = csv.writer(f)
            w.writerow(["n", "type", "level", "phase", "t_s", "tilt_deg", "rate_rads", "turns", "alt_m",
                        "hspeed_ms", "dist_m", "recovery", "p_rads", "q_rads", "r_rads"])
            w.writerows(rows)
        summary = {}
        for kind in args.types:
            for level in args.levels:
              for mode in modes:
                ee = [e for e in results if e["type"] == kind and e["level"] == level and e["recovery"] == mode]
                if ee:
                    rt = [e["recovery_time_s"] for e in ee if e["recovered"]]
                    key = f"{kind}/{level}/{mode}" if args.compare_recovery else f"{kind}/{level}"
                    summary[key] = {
                        "below_5m": int(sum(e["virtual_ground"] for e in ee)),
                        "mean_spin_stop_s": (float(np.mean([e["spin_stopped_s"] for e in ee if e["spin_stopped_s"] is not None]))
                                             if any(e["spin_stopped_s"] is not None for e in ee) else None),
                        "mean_lowest_m": float(np.mean([e["min_alt_m"] for e in ee])),
                        "impacts": len(ee), "recovered_pct": 100.0 * len(rt) / len(ee),
                        "mean_turns": float(np.mean([e["turns"] for e in ee])),
                        "mean_peak_rate_rads": float(np.mean([e["peak_rate_rads"] for e in ee])),
                        "mean_recovery_s": float(np.mean(rt)) if rt else None,
                        "mean_alt_loss_m": float(np.mean([e["alt_loss_m"] for e in ee])),
                        "lowest_m": float(np.min([e["min_alt_m"] for e in ee]))}
        json.dump({"date": stamp, "settings": vars(args), "disturbances_rads": DISTURBANCES, "turns": TURNS,
                   "limits_original": original, "limit_off_original": free,
                   "limit_off_values": {k: FREE_VALUES[k] for k in free}, "climb_mps": CLIMB, "rate_rule": {"version": RULE_VERSION, "k": RULE_K, "max_rate": RULE_MAX,
                   "thr_inv_x_hover": RULE_THR_INV, "alpha": RULE_ALPHA, "up_min": RULE_UP_MIN, "brake": RULE_BRAKE,
                   "side_k": RULE_SIDE_K, "side_max": RULE_SIDE_MAX}, "impact_mode": bool(limits), "hover_alt_m": ALT,
                   "virtual_ground_m": FLOOR, "cut_margin_m": MARGIN,
                   "hit": HIT, "lead_deg": LEAD_DEG if HIT == "lead" else None, "lead_ff_off": _FF is not None, "brake_mss": BRAKE, "yaw_brake_t": YAW_BRAKE_T,
                   "criterion": {"rate_rads": RATE_OK, "tilt_deg": TILT_OK, "alt_m": ALT_OK,
                                 "hspeed_ms": HSPEED_OK, "held_s": HELD_S, "max_s": RECOVER_MAX_S},
                   "summary": summary, "impacts": results}, open(f"{base}.json", "w"), indent=2, default=str)
        if summary:
            w0 = 30 if args.compare_recovery else 14
            print("\n📊 SUMMARY" + (" - same hits, recoveries compared" if args.compare_recovery
                                    else " (baseline = ArduPilot recovers by itself)"))
            print(f"{'Type/level' + ('/recovery' if args.compare_recovery else ''):<{w0}}{'Recovered':>10}{'Turns':>8}"
                  f"{'Peak rate':>12}{'Recovery':>10}{'Height loss':>13}{'Lowest':>9}{'Avg low':>9}"
                  f"{'Spin stop':>11}{'Below 5 m':>11}")
            for k, s in summary.items():
                rec = f"{s['mean_recovery_s']:.2f} s" if s["mean_recovery_s"] is not None else "-"
                sst = f"{s['mean_spin_stop_s']:.2f} s" if s["mean_spin_stop_s"] is not None else "-"
                print(f"{k:<{w0}}{s['recovered_pct']:>9.0f}%{s['mean_turns']:>8.1f}{s['mean_peak_rate_rads']:>8.1f} rad/s"
                      f"{rec:>10}{s['mean_alt_loss_m']:>11.1f} m{s['lowest_m']:>7.1f} m{s['mean_lowest_m']:>7.1f} m"
                      f"{sst:>11}"
                      f"{str(s['below_5m']) + '/' + str(s['impacts']):>11}")
            print(f"Virtual ground {FLOOR:g} m: going below it counts as FAILED (the real floor is never reached)")
        print(f"\nSaved: {base}.csv / _steps.csv / .json")
        link.land(timeout=120)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--types", nargs="+", default=["flip", "spin", "bird"], choices=list(DISTURBANCES))
    ap.add_argument("--levels", nargs="+", default=LEVELS, choices=LEVELS)
    ap.add_argument("--repeats", type=int, default=1)
    ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
    ap.add_argument("--keep-limits", action="store_true", help="do not touch the ATC_ACCEL limits")
    ap.add_argument("--compare-recovery", action="store_true",
                    help="fly every impact once per test (same hit): ardupilot / limit-on / limit-off / "
                         "spin-stop-fast / rate-rule")
    ap.add_argument("--modes", nargs="+", choices=RECOVERIES, default=None,
